# SET 1 — Question 10: Random Access, Proverbs 3
*Read the verse; give the reference.*

A) Proverbs 3:2  B) Proverbs 3:4  C) Proverbs 3:6  D) Proverbs 3:3

**7.** "Do not be wise in your own eyes; Fear the LORD and depart from evil."

A) Proverbs 3:12  B) Proverbs 3:14  C) Proverbs 3:9  D) Proverbs 3:11

# SET 4 — Question 10: Random Access, Matthew 6

A) Matthew 6:2  B) Matthew 6:1  C) Matthew 6:8  D) Matthew 6:9

**3.** "But when you do a charitable deed, do not let your left hand know what your right hand is doing,"
A) Matthew 6:5  B) Matthew 6:3  C) Matthew 6:6  D) Matthew 6:2

A) Matthew 6:4  B) Matthew 6:6  C) Matthew 6:3  D) Matthew 6:5

**5.** "And when you pray, you shall not be like the hypocrites. For they love to pray standing in the synagogues and on the corners of the streets, that they may be seen by men. Assuredly, I say to you, they have their reward."
A) Matthew 6:8  B) Matthew 6:3  C) Matthew 6:5  D) Matthew 6:1

**6.** "But you, when you pray, go into your room, and when you have shut your door, pray to your Father who is in the secret place; and your Father who sees in secret will reward you openly."

# SET 12 — Question 6: Geographical Location
*Every passage that carries a location tag, asked in both directions.*

**1.** Where does **Exodus 4:1-15** take place?
A) The Jordan Valley, in the Wilderness of Judea  B) Jerusalem, in Judah  C) Midian, at the Mountain of God  D) Jerusalem and Nazareth

**2.** Where does **Exodus 33:17-19** take place?

**4.** Where does **Deuteronomy 1:5-9** take place?
A) Jerusalem and the Plain of Ono  B) Plains of Moab, East of the Jordan  C) Antioch in Pisidia, in the Synagogue  D) Gilgal, in the Land of Canaan

**5.** Where does **Deuteronomy 5:32-33** take place?
A) Capernaum and the Towns of Galilee  B) A Level Place in Galilee  C) Plains of Moab, East of the Jordan  D) Jerusalem, at the House of God

# SET 15 — Question 1: Theme of the Passage
*One question for each of the 108 passages.*

**1.** What is the theme of **Exodus 4:1-15**?
A) God equips the reluctant servant  B) Righteousness before an unsearchable God  C) The works of God and the light of the world  D) An outsider confesses the true God

**2.** What is the theme of **Exodus 33:17-19**?
A) Grace and mercy freely given  B) Integrity, mercy, and the peril of pride  C) Refusing distraction and intimidation  D) Praise to the good and everlasting King

**3.** What is the theme of **Leviticus 24:14-16**?
A) Walking straight in the way of the LORD  B) The God who sees, instructs, and defends  C) Reverence for the name of God  D) Wisdom learned in the house of mourning

**4.** What is the theme of **Deuteronomy 1:5-9**?
A) Peacemaking and reconciled brothers  B) Inheritance granted by God's command  C) Moving forward at God's command  D) God's righteousness received, not self-established

**5.** What is the theme of **Deuteronomy 5:32-33**?
A) Walking straight in the way of the LORD  B) Holding to God's way in the dark  C) The fear of the LORD and wisdom's call  D) The LORD hovering over His city

**6.** What is the theme of **Joshua 1:1-9**?
A) The works of God and the light of the world  B) Courage grounded in the word and presence of God  C) An outsider confesses the true God  D) The fear of the LORD and wisdom's call

**7.** What is the theme of **Joshua 2:8-11**?
A) An outsider confesses the true God  B) The king's city set on the height  C) Reverence for the name of God  D) God feeding the exhausted servant

**8.** What is the theme of **Joshua 17:3-7**?
A) God's righteousness received, not self-established  B) Inheritance granted by God's command  C) God's regard for the poor and contrite  D) The blessing of God's correction

**9.** What is the theme of **Joshua 22:21-32**?
A) Prayer and perseverance under mockery  B) Peacemaking and reconciled brothers  C) God's regard for the poor and contrite  D) Praise to the good and everlasting King
